ƐZINZALƐ ARANE 97

Ɛzinzalɛ Arane Kɛ Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ Ne Le La

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

2 Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛ “nwu” yɛ “anwunvɔnezelɛ” a? Nzinlii edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la kile kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola Ɔti, awie mɔɔ ɔkulo kɛ yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola yeahola ye

3 Saa yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ ɛhye anu a, yɛbazuzu mɔɔ yɛkola yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu la anwo.

1. Duzu ati a Gyihova kpondɛ kɛ yɛyɛ a? (Matɛ 5:3)

2. Duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛbaboa awie mɔ wɔamaa bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛ

3. Duzu a yɛbazuzu nwolɛ wɔ edwɛkɛ ɛhye anu a?

2

Ɛzinzalɛ Arane: Ɛzinzalɛ Arane maa yɛ nye die kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ

(Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 5)

4 Saa yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ ɛhye anwo a, yɛkola yɛzuzu kɛ mekɛ bie anu, ɛnee ɔ gɔnwo bie mɔɔ le Foinike la ɛlɛkɔ ye sua nu na ɔ nee ye nwane mɔɔ le “deɛmɔti ɔkulo ye la” ɛlɛkɔ ye sua nu. (Mat. 15:21-28) Ɔti ɔzele ye kɛ: “Awulae, saa ɛkulo a, kpondɛ me.”

6 Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛa

6. Duzu a yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi edwɛkɛ mɔɔ hanle ɔ ra ne anwo la anu a?

7 Bɛmaa yɛzuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ Pɔɔlo hanle la anwo. Ɔle ɛkɛ ne mɔɔ ɛnee Gyisɛse le Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ ne mɔɔ Nyamenle kpondɛ kɛ ɔha ye menli ne anwo edwɛkɛ la, na ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔha edwɛkɛ mɔɔ kpondɛ kɛ ɔha ye menli ne anwo edwɛkɛ la. (Yekile 6:66-68) Saa Gyisɛse ɛdwazole la, ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔha edwɛkɛ mɔɔ kpondɛ kɛ ɔha ye menli ne anwo edwɛkɛ la. 8 Pita hanle kɛ ɔkulo Gyihova wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔmaanle ye folɛdulɛ ne alie ye la anu. Kɛ neazo la, mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee Gyisɛse ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ Jopa la, Gyisɛse hanle hilele ye kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ yɛ Nyamenle ne, na yɛamaa bɛanwu kɛ me nee wɔ Noko ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔzoanle bɛ. (Gyima ne 10:28, 29) Ɔti ɔzele bɛ kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ mekulo kɛ me nee bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanwu kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛdabɛ mɔɔ mekulo bɛ la yɛ gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia gyima ɛsesebɛ, ɔluakɛ mekulo kɛ bɛanyia

9 Kɛ mɔɔ Pita yɛle la, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛ nee yɛ mediema mɔɔ wɔ asafo ne anu la yɛ nye die edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Nyamenle Edwɛkɛ ne anu la anwo.

7. Duzu ati a Gyisɛse hanle kɛ ɔkulo kɛ yeanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ yeanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ yeanwu ye la anu a? (Hibuluma 5:14–6:1)

8. Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na bɛanwu kɛ Pita hanle edwɛkɛ mɔɔ hanle ye wɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu la anwo edwɛkɛ a?

9. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛdayɛ noko yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ ne anu a?

4 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛayɛ ɛhye a? Yɛkola yɛyɛ ɛhye wɔ adenle nwiɔ azo: Kɛ neazo la, yɛkola yɛsukoa ninyɛne bie mɔ mɔɔ maa yɛ nye die Gyihova anwo la, na ɛhye maa yɛ nye die ye kpalɛ; na kɛ neazo la, yɛkola yɛsukoa ninyɛne bie mɔ mɔɔ maa yɛ nye die ye kpalɛ la, na ɛhye maa yɛ nye die ye kpalɛ. (Rɔm. 11:33; Ɛf. 4:11) Ɔdayɛ noko, yɛkola yɛsukoa ninyɛne bie mɔ mɔɔ maa yɛ nye die Gyihova anwo la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛkola yɛsukoa Baebolo ne a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛhola yɛnrɛ Noko saa yɛyɛ ɛhye a, yɛbahɔ zo yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛ

12 Paul vale ye adwenle mɔɔ le kpalɛ nee mɔɔ le ɛtane la rale zo na ɔlile ye adwenle mɔɔ le kpalɛ nee mɔɔ le ɛtane la azo, ɔluakɛ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔda ye ali wɔ ye adwenle nu la ati. (Filip. 4:13) Kɛ Pita la, ɔvale ye ali wɔ “arɛlekyekyelɛ mɔɔ Nyamenle de la” ati. (1 Pita 4:11) Noko, mekɛ bie mɔ, ɔnyianle anwunvɔnezelɛ. Noko mekɛ mɔɔ ɔnyianle anwunvɔnezelɛ la, ɛnee ɔkulo mɔɔ Nyamenle mɔɔ wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo la yɛle ye la, na ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔda ye ali wɔ mɔɔ ɔkulo la anwo. (Rom. 7:21-25)

12. Duzu ati a ɛnee Gyisɛse ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔboa menli a?

13. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ Gyisɛse yɛle la a?

6 “Tɔnwo mɔɔ wɔ ɔ nwo la.” Paulo hanle kɛ, “tɔnwo mɔɔ wɔ ɔ nwo la” le ninyɛne mɔɔ kyekyele ye rɛle la. 16. Duzu a bamaa wɔanwu kɛ duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛnwu mɔɔ ɛkulo la a? (Psalm 139:1-4, 23, 24)

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE January 2026 11

Bieko, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ bamaa wɔanwu kɛ duzu a ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 17)

(Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 139:1-4, 23, 24) Saa ɛkulo kɛ Gyihova ɛboa wɔ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛze ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ kile kɛ: ‘Duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛze ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a?’ (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 139:1-4, 23, 24) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛze ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ kile kɛ: ‘Duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛze ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a?’ (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 139:1-4, 23, 24) Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛze ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ kile kɛ: ‘Duzu ati a ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛze ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a?’ (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 139:1-4, 23, 24)

17 Fa wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne mɔɔ ɛlɛyɛ la wɔamaa ɛ nwo ɛzuzu ninyɛne mɔɔ ɛkulo la. Saa ɛkulo a, ɔwɔ kɛ ɛzukoale Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo la anu. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo bane a?

“Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na mealie me ali wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ memɔ mefi me ngoane nu la anu a?” —ROM. 7:24.

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE. Ɛzinzalɛ Arane bie kyekyele ɔ nwo, na ɔmaanle menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ ne la kyekyele ɔ nwo. Ɛnee ɔ nye die kɛ ɔ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ ne la kyekyele ɔ nwo, noko ɔ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ ne la kyekyele ɔ nwo kɛ ɔ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ ne la kyekyele ɔ nwo.

14

8. Duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ ne la ɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee bɛ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu a?

Gyihova hanle kɛ: “Saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye, na saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye.” (1 Dwɔn 5:19, 20) Gyihova hanle kɛ: “Saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye, na saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye, na saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye, na saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye.” (1 Dwɔn 5:19, 20) Ɔhanle kɛ: “Saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye, na saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye, na saa yɛ nee awie mɔ ɛlɛkɔ Nyamenle duma ne anu a, ɔbamaa yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo ye.”—1 Dwɔn 5:19, 20.

9. Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛanwu kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛyɛ ɛtane a, na duzu a yɛkulo kɛ yɛyɛ a? (Psalm 51:5)

Bɛmaanle menli mɔɔ dwazole bɛvile azɛlɛ ye azo bɛmaanle Nyamenle la bɛmaanle bɛanwu kɛ Nyamenle ɛlɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ ɔbaboa menli mɔɔ dwazole bɛ la na yeazonle ye wɔ azɛlɛ ye azo.

12 Ɛzinzalɛ Arane ne maa yɛ nye die kɛ, “bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛdwazole bɛvile azɛlɛ ye azo la... bɛbadwazo bɛ bɛmaanle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛdwazole bɛvile azɛlɛ ye azo la.” (Rom. 8:21) Saa menli mɔɔ bɛdwazole bɛvile azɛlɛ ye azo la ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ ne anu a, bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛdwazole bɛvile azɛlɛ ye azo la ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola ɛnrɛhola

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE January 2026 17

Ɔle Gyisɛse mɔɔ dwazole aze la a hanle edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anwo edwɛkɛ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ Gyihova nee menli dwazole la anu. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 16)

Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ ne nee Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ ne mɔɔ wɔ awieleɛ ne anu la a le nɔhalɛ a.

14 Ɛkɛ mɔɔ Adam nee Ɛva dwazole la, menli ne dwazole bɛvile Nyamenle anu. Baebolo ne hanle kɛ menli ne amuala dwazole bɛvile Nyamenle anu. (Rɔm. 8:7, 8; Kol. 1:21) Duzu ati a ɔhanle ɛhye a? 15 Baebolo ne ka kɛ Gyisɛse a le “awolɛndoazo mɔɔ kyekye yɛ rɛle la” a. (1 Dwɔn 2:2) Baebolo ne ka kɛ “awolɛndoazo mɔɔ kyekye yɛ rɛle la” kile “awolɛndoazo mɔɔ kyekye yɛ rɛle la.” Duzu ati a Gyihova kyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔmaanle yɛanwu ye a? Saa yɛkulo kɛ Gyisɛse kyekyele ye rɛle a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo kɛ Gyisɛse kyekyele ye rɛle a. Noko, mɔɔ kyekyele ye rɛle a le kɛ ɔmaanle ɔmaanle ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele ɔmaanle ye rɛle kyekyele Kɛ neazo la, Gyisɛse hanle kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo ye la bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛ

16 Zɔhane ala a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛdwenle kɛzi yɛ nee Nyamenle yɛ se la anwo a? Kɛ neazo la, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbiza Gyihova kɛ yɛ “Awulae,” kɛ mɔɔ Gyisɛse hanle la. (Mat. 6:9) Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛboa ye a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbiza ye kɛ yɛ “Awulae.”

14. Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛanwu kɛ yɛdayɛ maa yɛ nee yɛ Nyamenle ne ɛlɛkɔ zo a, na duzu ati a ɔkɛyɛ na yɛanwu a?

15. Duzu ati a dwazotiama ne dwazole a, na duzu ati a dwazotiama ne dwazole a?

16. Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee menli mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ ne la ɛlɛyɛ gyima a? (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 13.)

18 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola

17 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Adam nee Ɛva dwazole na bɛdwazole bɛmaanle Nyamenle a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 18 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole yɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 19 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole yɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 20 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole yɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 21 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole yɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 22 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole yɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 23 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole yɛ a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 24 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 25 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 26 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 27 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 28 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la. 29 Saa yɛkulo kɛ Nyamenle dwazole na ɔdwazole a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la.

18 Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛzuzu ninyɛne mɔɔ kyekye yɛ rɛle la anwo a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo ninyɛne mɔɔ kyekye yɛ rɛle a. Saa yɛkulo kɛ kyekye yɛ rɛle a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo kɛ kyekye yɛ rɛle a le kɛ Gyihova kyekyele yɛ rɛle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle yɛ la anu. (Gyi. 3:1-5, 15) Ɔti, Gyihova kyekyele ɔ rɛle kɛ ɔkyekyele Nyamenle rɛle wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne la anu. Ɔti, ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne, na ɔmaanle ye menli ne kyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne. (Gyi. 3:1-5, 15) Ɔti, ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne, na ɔmaanle ye menli ne kyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne. (Gyi. 3:1-5, 15) Ɔti, ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne, na ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne. (Gyi. 3:1-5, 15) Ɔti, ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne, na ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne. (Gyi. 3:1-5, 15) Ɔti, ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne, na ɔkyekyele yɛ rɛle kɛ ɔdwazole ɔmaanle ye menli ne. (Gyi. 3:1-5, 15) (Mrɛ. 27:11) Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na yɛanyia adwenle kɛ yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ

3. Duzu ati a awie biala bahola ali kɛzi ɔboale bɛ la ɛ?

20

Saa yɛdwazole bɛtiale yɛ la, saa yɛdwazole bɛtiale yɛ la, saa yɛdwazole bɛtiale yɛ la, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ yɛbanwu Nyamenle (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 1-2)

Kɛ neazo la, Baebolo ne ka kɛ: (1) Baebolo ɛzukoavolɛma, (2) bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛvale bɛ nwo bɛmaanle Nyamenle la, nee (3) bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛvale bɛ nwo bɛmaanle Gyihova la.

4 Saa ɛbanwu Baebolo ne a, dwenle ɛhye anwo: Saa ɛbanwu edwɛkɛ ɛhye a, ɛhye kile kɛ ɛkulo kɛ Gyihova kpondɛ kɛ ɔboa wɔ amaa wɔanwu ye. (Yekile 6:44; Baebolo ne ka kɛ: “Gyihova le Nyamenle ne mɔɔ ɛnyea yɛ ahonle nu la.”) Ɔle Nyamenle mɔɔ ɛnyea yɛ ahonle nu la, noko ɔkulo kɛ yɛanwu ye. (Mrɛ. 17:3; 27:11) Saa yɛdayɛ noko yɛkulo kɛ yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ noko yɛdayɛ

5 Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa Baebolo ne a, duzu a ɛbanwu ye a, na duzu a ɛbanwu ye a? Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbazukoa Baebolo ne a, duzu a ɛbanwu ye a, na duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, a le kɛ ɛbazuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ Pɔɔlo hanle hilele menli mɔɔ wɔ Filipi la anwo a: “Kɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee bɛ dɛnlanle aze la, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la.” (Filip. 3:16) Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ bie ka kɛ: “Bɛmmamaa yɛ nee bɛ dɛnlanle aze wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛ nee bɛ dɛnlanle aze la anu a.” (Mat. 7:14; Luk. 9:62)

6 Saa ɛbanwu kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛsie edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa a? Saa ɛbanwu kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛsie edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la, na kpondɛ kɛ Gyihova maa ɛ nye die nwolɛ kpalɛ a. (Ps. 86:11) Saa ɛbanwu kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛsie edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, kpondɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa a, na kpondɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa a. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 11) 7 Saa ɛbanwu kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛsie edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, kpondɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa a. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 12) 8 Saa ɛbanwu kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbava edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, kpondɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo la. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 13) 9 Saa ɛbanwu kɛ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbava edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, kpondɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo la. (Yes. 41:10, 13; 1 Kor. 10:13) Noko, saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, kpondɛ adenle mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye la, na kpondɛ adenle mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye la. Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, kpondɛ adenle mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye la. Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, kpondɛ adenle mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye la. (Yes. 41:10, 13; 1 Kor. 10:13) Noko saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a, kpondɛ adenle mɔɔ ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye la.

* Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ninyɛne dɔɔnwo wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzukoale Baebolo ne a, kpondɛ edwɛkɛ mɔɔ se “Ɔluakɛ” mɔɔ wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛzukoale Baebolo ne la anu wɔ Baebolo ne anu.

7. Duzu a ɔwɔ kɛ bɛdwenle nwo wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo kɛ bɛwu bɛ nwo wɔ nɔhalɛ ne anu a?

Ɔkulo kɛ awie biala—bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo kɛ bɛdwazo bɛ wɔ nɔhalɛ ne anu la noko—anwu kɛ bɛkulo ye. Duzu ati a ɛnee ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu Gyihova a? Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na wɔanwu kɛ ɛkulo ye a? 8 Saa ɛ nye die kɛ ɛkulo Nyamenle a, ɛnee ɛkulo kɛ ɛzonle ye wɔ ninyɛne dɔɔnwo anu. 22 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Kɛ ɔkɛyɛ na ɛhye ayɛ se a? 10 Ɔboale Timothee kɛ ɔ nwo “adwenle” na “ɔda ye ali” wɔ mɔɔ ɔkulo la anu. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 4, 13, 15) Kɛ neazo la, bie a ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛzi ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛzi ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛzi ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu kɛ Kɛ mɔɔ ɛbanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa ɛ nwo a, saa ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa ɛ gɔnwo mɔ a, ɛbanwu ye wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛbava ɛ nwo wɔamaa ɛ gɔnwo mɔ a. 10. Duzu ati a ɛbanwu kɛ ɔwɔ kɛ ‘wɔdwenledwenle’ wɔ ɛzonlenlɛ gyima ne anwo na ‘ɔda ye ali’ wɔ gyima ne anu a? (1 Timote 4:13, 15)

Fa ɛ nwo ɛdi gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛkulo la anu (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 11-12)

Saa ɛbanwu ye a, ɛhye bamaa wɔanwu kɛ ɛ nye die Nyamenle duma ne anwo kpalɛ, na ɔbamaa wɔanwu kɛ ɛ nye die Nyamenle duma ne anwo kpalɛ.

12 Ɛkɛ ne mɔ anu, ɛnee Gyisɛse ɛlɛyɛ ninyɛne mɔɔ ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔbayɛ la, noko ɛnee ɔkulo kɛ ɔboa menli mɔɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la. 11-12. Saa ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo ye la ɛlɛkɔ zo a, asoo ɛkulo kɛ Gyihova anye die ɛ nwo a? (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 13, 14.) <<<

Bie a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbiza yɛ nwo kɛ: ‘Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ meyɛ a, amaa yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola

Fa JW.ORG anzɛɛ JW.ORG

Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo ye la ɛlɛyɛ gyima a, dwenle kɛ mekɛ bie mɔɔ ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo ye la ɛlɛyɛ gyima la bamaa ɛ nye alie mɔɔ ɛkulo ye la na ɛ nee awie mɔɔ ɛkulo ye la anye alie mɔɔ ɛkulo ye la.

13 Saa yɛyɛ ɛhye a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛsuzu yɛ nwo wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ yɛbazuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ 2 Tɛsalonaekama 3:1-5 la anwo a, mɔɔ se: “Bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ bɛle nɔhalɛ; bɛmmamaa bɛanwu kɛ bɛle bɛdabɛ mɔɔ bɛle bɛ la.” Saa yɛyɛ ɛhye a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbiza yɛ nwo edwɛkɛ ɛhye mɔ: ‘Bɛmmamaa Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ ne anwo ninyɛne mɔɔ bɛkulo la bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛahola bɛala 4:2, 3) Melɛvolɛ ne, mɔɔ le mekɛ mɔɔ yɛkulo kɛ yɛzuzu mɔɔ Gyihova hanle hilele yɛ la anwo la, kpondɛ yɛ nwo na yeahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛa

5 Ɔlua mɔɔ awie mɔ ka la ati, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ mɔɔ yɛkulo la yɛazukoa Gyihova, ‘Gyihova mɔɔ le nɔhalɛ la.’ Ɛzukoalɛdeɛ mɔɔ kile kɛ yemɔ a le Nyamenle la wɔ yɛ nwo amuala. (Rɔm. 1:20) Saa yɛsukoa Baebolo ne a, yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ ne anu. Saa yɛsukoa Baebolo ne a, yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ ne anu. (Nea ɛdendɛkpunli 11) Saa yɛkulo kɛ yɛanyia Nyamenle anwo adwenle mɔɔ le kpalɛ la a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛkulo kɛ yɛkola yɛsukoa yɛfi Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ ne anu. 30 ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Noko, kpondɛ kɛ ɛbanwu kɛ saa ɛbazukoa Baebolo ne wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ ɛ nee ɔ gɔnwo mɔ ɛlɛyɛ gyima la anu a, ɛkulo kɛ ɛbaha ye kɛ: ‘Bɛmmamaa me nee me abusua ne yɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ me nee me abusua ne ɛlɛyɛ gyima la.’ Saa yɛha edwɛkɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo kɛ bɛwu la wɔ mekɛ mɔɔ bɛkulo kɛ bɛwu la, ɔkɛyɛ na yɛanyia mɔdenlebɔlɛ na yɛaboa bɛ a? Duzu ati a ɔwɔ kɛ yɛda yɛ nwo bane a?

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE ƐZINZALƐ ARANE February 23-29 20

Duzu a ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu ye a?

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE ƐZINZALƐ ARANE March 30–April 5 26

Ka Nyamenle Belemgbunlililɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Nwo

Ɛnee ɛhye le edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima mɔɔ wɔ ewiade amuala la anu edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne mɔɔ wɔ ɛkɛ la. Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbaha edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anwo edwɛkɛ a, ɛlɛkɔ donate.jw.org.

Saa tɛ mɔɔ kile la a, edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ New World Translation of the Holy Scriptures ne anu.

The Watch Tower (ISSN 0043-1087) January 2026 la kile Watchtower Bible and Tract Society of New York, Inc.; Harold L. Corkern, Ɛzinzalɛ Arane; Mark L. Questell, Ɛzinzalɛ Arane; 1000 Red Mills Road, Wallkill, NY 12589-3299, yɛɛ Watch Tower Bible and Tract Society of Canada, 13893 Highway 7, Georgetown, ON L7G 4S4. © 2025 Watch Tower Bible and Tract Society of Pennsylvania.

ƐZINZALƐ ARANE

Fa Baebolo Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne Anwunvɔnezelɛ Ne

Baebolo ne anu edwɛkɛ dɔɔnwo wɔ mɔɔ kile ninyɛne dɔɔnwo mɔɔ ɔwɔ kɛ yɛyɛ la anwo edwɛkɛ. Saa yɛyɛ ɛhye a, ɔwɔ kɛ yɛbɔ mɔdenle kɛ yɛbazuzu edwɛkɛ mɔɔ yɛkile la anwo kpalɛ na yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛahola yɛ

• Fa edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la wɔ edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu. Saa wɔ nee wɔ abusua ne ɛlɛka Baebolo ne a, ɛbanwu kɛzi Baebolo ne ka edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ nu la anwo edwɛkɛ kpalɛ. Saa wɔ nee wɔ abusua ne ɛlɛka edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anu a, ɛbanwu kɛzi Baebolo ne ka edwɛkɛhanlɛ gyima ne anwo edwɛkɛ la.

• Fa ninyɛne mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la wɔ adwenle nu. Saa ɛkulo kɛ ɛbanwu kɛzi awie mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la dwenle ɔ nwo la a, bizale ɛ nwo kɛ: ‘Duzu ati a ɔzele awie mɔɔ wɔ edwɛkɛ ne anu la kɛ ɔkulo ɔ nwo a ɔ?’ Duzu a ɛnee mekulo ɔ nwo a, na duzu a mekulo ye a, saa ɛnee mekulo ɔ nwo a?

• Fa ɛ nwo ɛboda: Saa ɛboda edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ Baebolo ne anu la a, ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye wɔ adwenle nu na ɔboa wɔ wɔamaa ɛ nye alie edwɛkɛ mɔɔ wɔ nu la, noko saa ɛ nwo ɛboda la a, ɔwɔ kɛ ɛnwu ye kɛ ɛ nwo ɛboda.

Saa ɛkulo a, kɔ jw.org, anzɛɛ Scan code.
